## Complete Orchestrator Agent Architecture Analysis

Based on my thorough review of the codebase, here's how the orchestrator agent works:

### 1. **Orchestrator-Subagent Interaction Model**

The orchestrator does NOT wait for all subagent results before synthesizing. Instead, it **uses incremental streaming with live subagent delegation**:

**Key Flow:**
- **Orchestrator (Python backend)** runs in a TypeScript sidecar container using the Claude Agent SDK
- When the user asks a question, the orchestrator calls the `Task` tool to delegate work to named subagents:
  - `sql-analyst`: for data queries
  - `chart-builder`: for visualizations
- The orchestrator continues generating text and can make multiple subagent calls **concurrently or sequentially** as needed
- The orchestrator's final response synthesizes subagent outputs incrementally

**Critical SDK Behavior** (from project memory):
- The SDK does NOT yield the subagent's **final** assistant message to the caller
- The subagent's actual output lives in `tool_use_result.content` on the `UserMessage` — a list of content blocks
- Intermediate subagent messages ARE yielded with `parent_tool_use_id` set
- Stream events for subagent turns may NOT be yielded by the SDK

### 2. **Streaming Architecture - Three-Layer SSE Pipeline**

```
Frontend UI
    ↓ (fetch /api/chat)
Backend (FastAPI) - stream_chat()
    ↓ (await create_future, health check, then stream POST)
Sidecar (Express.js) - /query endpoint
    ↓ (instantiate Claude SDK query iterator)
Claude Agent SDK
    ↓ (iterate messages from CLI process)
Sidecar streams back SSE
    ↓ (process, create Langfuse traces, transform)
Backend parses SSE, extracts chart specs, synthesizes events
    ↓ (yield transformed SSE)
Frontend receives SSE, updates UI incrementally
```

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts` (lines 17-282)

The frontend `streamSSE()` function:

1. **SSE Parsing** (lines 42-86):

   - Handles partial lines and buffer edge cases

2. **Event Handling** (lines 175-282, `handleSSEEvent`):
   - `thinking` / `answer`: calls `onTextChunk(text)` → accumulates in buffer → flushed every 50ms
   - `thinking_done`: switches phase, flushes current text as thinking segment
   - `tool_call`: creates pending tool segment (shows input immediately)
   - `tool_result`: merges result into pending segment (adds output columns/rows/chart)
   - `subagent_start`: creates subagent_start segment (shows "SQL analyst working...")
   - `subagent_end`: creates subagent_end segment with optional chart_spec array
   - `user_question`: pauses and shows user question dialog
   - `done` / `error`: finalizes streaming

3. **Context Management** (AgentContext.tsx, lines 1-612):
   - **Message segments** track different content types (thinking, answer, tool calls, subagents, errors)
   - **Text buffer flushing** (lines 37-48): 50ms debounce prevents excessive re-renders
   - **Phase tracking** (lines 33, 117): switches from 'thinking' → 'answer' at `thinking_done`
   - **Subagent callbacks** (lines 180-220):
     - `onSubagentStart`: pushes segment, shows subagent name
     - `onSubagentEnd`: pushes segment with `chart_spec` (Plotly data/layout)
   - **Chart rendering** (MessageBubble.tsx, lines 323-333):
     - Filters segments with `chart_spec` into chart widgets
     - Strips duplicate chart JSON code blocks from answer text (lines 18-20)
     - Renders via `ChartWidget` component (Plotly)

### 6. **Chart Spec Capture Mechanism**

The system captures chart specs via **direct input inspection**, not text parsing:

**Backend capture** (agent.py, lines 152-184):
```python
def _build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages(msg, tool_names, subagent_chart_specs):
    parent_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
    if parent_id and tool_names.get(parent_id) == "chart-builder":
        # Inspect assistant message for render_chart tool_use blocks
        for block in msg.get("message", {}).get("content", []):
            if block.get("type") == "tool_use" and "render_chart" in block.get("name", ""):
                spec = block.get("input", {})  # Direct input, not parsed from text
                # Validate data is present
                # Accumulate in subagent_chart_specs[parent_id]
```

**Why this matters:**
- Plotly specs contain nested JSON that would be malformed if extracted from free text
- Direct input inspection avoids double-encoding/parsing errors
- Multiple charts from one subagent are accumulated in array

### 7. **Tool Result Handling**

**MCP Tool Implementation** (mcp_sse.py, lines 26-167):

1. **execute_sql** (lines 103-117):
   - Executes DuckDB query asynchronously
   - Returns JSON: `{"status": "success", "columns", "rows": [...100], "rowCount"}`
   - On error: `{"status": "error", "error": "..."}`

2. **render_chart** (lines 131-163):
   - Validates data traces are non-empty
   - Returns minimal acknowledgement (spec captured at tool_use input level)
   - Skips specs with all-empty traces

3. **ask_user_question** (lines 118-130):
   - Stores question in session store
   - Blocks until user responds (async wait)
   - Returns user's answer or timeout

### 8. **Session & Container Management**

- **Backend session ID** (`X-Session-ID` header): Stable key for:
  - DuckDB session isolation (per-user database)
  - Container reuse across requests
  - MCP SSE endpoint routing
  
- **Agent session ID** (`session_id`): Claude SDK's conversation session
  - Changes after first response
  - Used for resume with fallback to history
  
- **Container lifecycle**:
  - Created once per backend session
  - Reused for multiple agent turns
  - Cleaned up after `CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS` or app shutdown
  - Health check ensures readiness before queries

### 9. **Key Design Patterns**

| Pattern | Implementation |
|---------|-----------------|
| **Incremental synthesis** | Orchestrator delegates to subagents, streams intermediate text, then final answer |
| **SDK message filtering** | `parent_tool_use_id` prevents subagent tokens from leaking into orchestrator output |
| **Chart spec capture** | Direct input inspection of `tool_use` blocks, not text parsing |
| **Resume fallback** | Catches "No conversation found", retries with conversation history prepended |
| **Container stability** | Backend session ID ensures same container across requests |
| **SSE transformation** | Backend processes SDK stream, extracts charts, emits browser-friendly events |
| **Real-time UI** | Text buffered 50ms, segments pushed immediately for tool calls/subagents |

This architecture enables **true streaming with subagent parallelization** — the orchestrator can call multiple subagents, stream their outputs as they arrive, and the UI updates in real-time with tool inputs/outputs, thinking phases, and chart specs.

> AGENT

**No, it does not wait for all subagent results first.** The orchestrator streams incrementally.

Here's how it works:

1. **The orchestrator (Claude) decides** when to call subagents by issuing `Task` tool calls (e.g., to `sql-analyst` or `chart-builder`). It can call them sequentially or concurrently — that's up to Claude's reasoning.

2. **As each subagent runs**, intermediate tokens are streamed through the sidecar → backend → frontend SSE pipeline. The backend filters out subagent tokens from the orchestrator's output using `parent_tool_use_id` (`agent.py:~348-386`).

3. **When a subagent finishes**, its result (including chart specs) is captured and emitted as a `subagent_end` SSE event. The orchestrator then receives the tool result and continues reasoning.

4. **The orchestrator synthesizes incrementally** — it generates its final response text as tokens stream, interleaved with subagent delegations. It doesn't buffer everything and emit at the end.

The flow looks like:

```
Orchestrator starts thinking/streaming text
  → calls sql-analyst (subagent_start event)
    → sql-analyst runs, executes SQL, returns results
  ← subagent_end event (with data)
  → calls chart-builder (subagent_start event)  
    → chart-builder runs, renders chart
  ← subagent_end event (with chart_spec)
Orchestrator continues streaming its synthesis text
  → tokens arrive as answer events in real-time
```

The key insight: **Claude (the orchestrator LLM) controls the sequencing**. It naturally calls subagents as needed during its reasoning, gets results back via tool results, and weaves its final answer around them — all streamed token-by-token to the UI.

> DEVELOPER

I mean for final answer streaming back to ui, does orchestrator get all results from subagents first?

> AGENT

Yes, exactly. For the **final answer text**, the orchestrator must get all subagent results back first before it can synthesize and stream its response.

The Claude SDK enforces this — subagent calls are `tool_use` blocks. The orchestrator's turn pauses while waiting for each tool result. Only after the tool result comes back can the orchestrator continue generating tokens.

So the sequence is:

```
Orchestrator turn 1: thinking + tool_use (call sql-analyst)
  → paused, waiting for tool result
  → sql-analyst runs to completion
  → tool_result returned to orchestrator
Orchestrator turn 2: thinking + tool_use (call chart-builder)
  → paused, waiting for tool result
  → chart-builder runs to completion
  → tool_result returned to orchestrator
Orchestrator turn 3: streams final synthesis text
  → this is what the user sees as the "answer"
```

The orchestrator **cannot** stream its final answer while a subagent is still running — it needs the data to synthesize. Each `Task` tool call is blocking from the orchestrator's perspective.

What **is** streamed incrementally to the UI during the wait are the `subagent_start`/`subagent_end` events and intermediate status, so the user sees progress. But the actual answer text only flows once all delegated work is done.

> DEVELOPER

then currently for chart rendered in ui now, is it rendered from orchestrator?

> AGENT

No, charts are rendered directly from the **chart-builder subagent**, not from the orchestrator.

The backend intercepts the subagent's `render_chart` tool_use input during streaming and sends it to the UI before the orchestrator even gets the result back.

Specifically:

1. **Backend captures chart spec** from the chart-builder subagent's `tool_use` block input directly (`agent.py:~152-184`, `_build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages`)
2. **Backend emits `subagent_end` SSE event** with `chart_spec` attached (`agent.py:~501-510`)
3. **Frontend renders** charts from `subagent_end` segments that have `chart_spec`, via `ChartWidget` (Plotly) — in `MessageBubble.tsx`

So the flow is:

```
chart-builder subagent calls render_chart tool
  → backend inspects tool_use input, extracts Plotly spec
  → backend emits subagent_end SSE with chart_spec
  → frontend renders chart via ChartWidget

orchestrator never touches the chart spec
```

The orchestrator only receives a minimal text result from the `Task` tool (metadata like `agentId`, `usage`, `duration_ms`). It doesn't see or relay the chart data — it just knows the chart-builder finished.

Great question. You're right to notice the gap — the orchestrator **can't** reference the actual chart data in its synthesis because it only gets metadata back from the `Task` tool result.

What actually happens is more like:

1. The orchestrator tells chart-builder what to chart (e.g., "create a bar chart of revenue by quarter")
2. The chart-builder does it, but the orchestrator only gets back a generic success confirmation
3. The orchestrator's "synthesis" is based on **what it asked for**, not what was actually rendered

So the coherence relies on the orchestrator **trusting** that the subagent did what it was told. The final answer text says something like "As shown in the chart above, Q3 had the highest revenue..." based on the SQL data it got from sql-analyst, not from inspecting the chart output.

This means there's a potential coherence risk — if the chart-builder produced something unexpected or failed silently, the orchestrator's text wouldn't reflect that.

## Complete Subagent Result Flow Analysis

I've thoroughly analyzed the codebase. Here's exactly how subagent results flow back to the orchestrator:

---

### 1. SIDECAR (TypeScript, sidecar/src/server.ts)

**What the orchestrator receives as tool_result:**

When a Task tool completes in the Claude Agent SDK, the sidecar receives an `assistant` message from the SDK with `parent_tool_use_id` pointing to the Task ID. The sidecar then forwards this as a `user` message to the backend, which contains:

```javascript
// From line 441-512 of agent.py, the user message structure is:
{
  type: "user",
  message: {
    content: [
      {
        type: "tool_result",
        tool_use_id: "<task_tool_id>",
        content: [
          // This is crucial: tool_use_result.content from the SDK
          { type: "text", text: "..." }
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  tool_use_result: {
    // SDK attaches the subagent's actual final message here
    content: [
      { type: "text", text: "..." }
    ]
  }
}
```

**Critical insight from memory:** The SDK's Task `tool_result` metadata field (`agentId`, `usage`, `duration_ms`) is NOT the subagent's output. The actual output lives in `tool_use_result.content` on the `UserMessage`.

---

### 2. BACKEND (Python, backend/app/agent.py)

**`_build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages` function (lines 152-184):**

This function inspects assistant messages looking for `render_chart` tool calls:

```python
def _build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages(msg: dict, tool_names: dict[str, str], subagent_chart_specs: dict) -> None:
    """
    Looks for render_chart calls in assistant messages whose parent_tool_use_id 
    matches a previously-seen Task (chart-builder) tool call.
    
    When found:
    - Extracts the input directly (no JSON parsing needed)
    - Validates that traces have actual data
    - Stores in subagent_chart_specs[task_id] = [spec, ...]
    """
    parent_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
    if not parent_id or tool_names.get(parent_id) != "chart-builder":
        return
    
    # Scan for tool_use blocks with "render_chart" in the name
    for block in msg.get("message", {}).get("content", []):
        if block.get("type") == "tool_use" and "render_chart" in block.get("name", ""):
            spec = block.get("input", {})  # Plotly spec object
            # Validate data exists before storing
            ...
            subagent_chart_specs.setdefault(parent_id, []).append(spec)
            return
```

**Stream flow (lines 187-543):**

The backend streams chat responses as SSE events:

1. **`subagent_start` event** (line 427):
   ```json
   {
     "event": "subagent_start",
     "data": {
       "id": "<task_tool_id>",
       "name": "sql-analyst" or "chart-builder",
       "prompt": "<subagent_prompt>"
     }
   }
   ```

2. **Intermediate assistant messages** (lines 389-427):
   - Token-level streaming from `stream_event` messages with `parent_tool_use_id` matching a Task ID
   - Text deltas are accumulated in `subagent_texts[task_id]`
   - `_build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages()` extracts render_chart specs into `subagent_chart_specs[task_id]`

3. **`subagent_end` event** (line 510):
   ```json
   {
     "event": "subagent_end",
     "data": {
       "id": "<task_tool_id>",
       "name": "sql-analyst" or "chart-builder",
       "chart_specs": [
         {
           "data": [...],
           "layout": {...}
         }
       ]
     }
   }
   ```
   - `chart_specs` is an **array** for multi-chart support
   - Sent only for Task results (subagents), not for direct execute_sql tool calls

**Other SSE events emitted:**
- `tool_call`: Direct tool invocations (execute_sql, render_chart, Task)
- `tool_result`: Results from non-subagent tools
- `thinking`, `answer`: Text streaming from orchestrator
- `done`, `error`: Terminal states

---

### 3. FRONTEND TYPES (frontend/src/types.ts)

**ContentSegment union type** (lines 51-66):

```typescript
interface ContentSegment {
  type: 'thinking' | 'tool' | 'answer' | 'subagent_start' | 'subagent_end' | 'user_question' | 'error';
  text?: string;                           // For thinking, answer, subagent_start (prompt)
  toolResult?: ToolCallResult;             // For tool segments
  subagentId?: string;                     // For subagent_start/end
  subagentName?: string;                   // 'sql-analyst' | 'chart-builder'
  chart_spec?: {                           // For subagent_end with charts
    data: unknown[];                       // Plotly trace array
    layout?: Record<string, unknown>;      // Plotly layout
    frames?: unknown[];                    // For animated charts
  };
  questionData?: UserQuestionData;         // For user_question segments
  userAnswer?: string[];                   // User's selected options
  userFreeText?: string;                   // Free-text response
  errorMessage?: string;                   // For error segments
}
```

**ToolCallResult** also has optional `chart_spec` (lines 32-36):
```typescript
chart_spec?: {
  data: unknown[];
  layout?: Record<string, unknown>;
  frames?: unknown[];
}
```

---

### 4. FRONTEND SSE PARSING (frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts)

**`handleSSEEvent` function** (lines 175-282):

Parses `subagent_end` events:

```typescript
case 'subagent_end': {
  // Backend sends chart_specs (array) or legacy chart_spec (single)
  const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
  const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
  const chartSpecs = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);

  if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
    // Emit one callback PER chart (for multi-chart support)
    for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
      callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
        id: data.id,
        name: data.name,
        chart_spec: spec,  // Single spec per callback
      });
    }
    // Emit text result separately if present
    if (data.result) {
      callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
        id: data.id,
        name: data.name,
        result: data.result,
      });
    }
  } else {
    // No charts, just text result
    callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
      id: data.id,
      name: data.name,
      result: data.result,
    });
  }
  break;
}
```

**Key behavior:** Multiple `onSubagentEnd` callbacks can fire for a single `subagent_end` SSE event when `chart_specs` has multiple items.

---

### 5. FRONTEND CONTEXT HANDLING (frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx)

**`onSubagentEnd` callback** (lines 205-220 and 444-459):

```typescript
onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
  // Push a segment for each chart_spec
  segmentsRef.current.push({
    type: 'subagent_end',
    subagentId: data.id,
    subagentName: data.name,
    chart_spec: data.chart_spec,  // May be undefined
    text: data.result,             // May be undefined
  });
  // Update message with new segments
  setMessages((prev) =>
    prev.map((m) =>
      m.id === assistantId
        ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
        : m
    )
  );
},
```

Segments are created with both `chart_spec` and `text` fields. When multiple charts exist, multiple segments are pushed (one per chart call + one for text).

---

### 6. FRONTEND RENDERING (frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx)

**Chart rendering** (lines 323-333):

```typescript
// Chart segment (tool with chart_spec or subagent_end with chart_spec)
if ((seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && seg.chart_spec)) {
  return (
    <div key={`chart-${i}`} className="message-bubble__chart-in-answer">
      {seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult ? (
        <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult!} />
      ) : seg.chart_spec ? (
        <ChartWidget data={seg.chart_spec.data} layout={seg.chart_spec.layout} frames={seg.chart_spec.frames} />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Chart stripping logic** (lines 18-20, 212-223):

Text segments are filtered through `stripChartSpecBlocks()` to remove raw JSON blocks when charts are present:

```typescript
function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();
}
```

This prevents both the rendered chart AND raw JSON from appearing.

---

## Summary: End-to-End Data Path

1. **Sidecar receives** SDK assistant message with `parent_tool_use_id=<task_id>`
2. **Backend streams**:
   - `subagent_start` event with prompt
   - Intermediate token events from the subagent
   - `subagent_end` event with `chart_specs: [...]` array
3. **Frontend SSE parser**:
   - Receives `subagent_end` with `chart_specs` array
   - Fires `onSubagentEnd` callback multiple times (once per chart + once for text if present)
4. **AgentContext**:
   - Pushes segments with `type: 'subagent_end'`, `chart_spec`, and `text`
5. **MessageBubble**:
   - Filters segments with `chart_spec` into answer blocks
   - Renders each via `ChartWidget` (Plotly)
   - Strips raw JSON text from accompanying answer text
   - Non-chart text is kept separate from charts

Now I understand the full picture. Let me ask the key clarifying question:

Currently, the chart-builder subagent calls `render_chart` (an MCP tool), and the backend intercepts the Plotly spec from the tool_use input. The orchestrator never sees it — it only gets generic Task metadata back.

**Should the chart still be produced by the chart-builder subagent, or should we remove chart-builder entirely and have the orchestrator call `render_chart` directly?**

The core tension is: if the orchestrator needs to see chart data and place it coherently in its final answer, there are two paths:

1. **Keep chart-builder subagent** — but inject its chart spec into the tool_result that the orchestrator receives, so the orchestrator "sees" it and can reference it in synthesis
2. **Remove chart-builder subagent** — give the orchestrator direct access to `render_chart`, so it produces charts inline with its answer text

Which direction do you prefer?

    20→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    21→    tables = db.list_tables()
    22→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    23→
    24→Task tool usage (CRITICAL — you MUST follow these rules):
    25→- When using the Task tool, you MUST set the "subagent_type" parameter to one of the named agents listed below. NEVER use generic values like "general-purpose", "code-writer", or any other value.
    26→- For any data question that requires SQL queries, set subagent_type to "sql-analyst"
    27→- For any visualization, chart, or graph request, set subagent_type to "chart-builder"
    28→- Do NOT set the "model" parameter on the Task tool — the named agents already have models configured. Omit the model field entirely.
    29→- After the chart-builder returns, do NOT repeat the chart JSON specification in your response. The chart is rendered automatically. Simply describe what the visualization shows in plain language.
    30→- After the sql-analyst returns, do NOT repeat the data tables or numbers. Simply add brief commentary on what the data means.
    31→- Explain findings in plain language after getting results
    32→
    33→Identity:
    34→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    35→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    36→
    37→Clarification:
    38→- When the user's request is ambiguous or could be interpreted in multiple ways, use the mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question tool (NOT the native AskUserQuestion tool) to ask for clarification before proceeding.
    39→- Provide 2-4 clear, concise options for the user to choose from.
    40→- Each option should have a short label and optional description.
    41→- Only ask when genuinely needed — don't over-ask for trivial decisions.
    42→"""
    43→    if not tables:
    44→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    45→    else:

    47→        for table in tables:

    55→def _build_table_schemas(db: Database) -> str:
    56→    """Build a text description of all loaded table schemas for subagent prompts."""
    57→    tables = db.list_tables()
    58→    if not tables:
    59→        return "\nNo tables are currently loaded."

    69→    """Build AgentDefinition objects for the sql-analyst and chart-builder subagents."""

    94→        "- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.\n"
    95→        "- For multi-series data, group into separate traces.\n"

    97→        "- Keep the chart clean and readable.\n\n"

    99→        "- Box plots: compute lowerfence, q1, median, q3, upperfence per group in SQL. "
   100→        "Use trace type \"box\" with those pre-computed fields instead of a raw `y` array.\n"
   101→        "- Histograms: compute bin counts with width_bucket() or CASE in SQL, then "
   102→        "render as a bar chart with the bin edges as `x` and counts as `y`.\n"
   103→        "- Scatter / line with many rows: sample (ORDER BY random() LIMIT 200) or "
   104→        "aggregate (e.g. average per time bucket) so each trace has at most ~200 points.\n"
   105→        "- General rule: each trace should have at most ~200 data points. "

   107→        "When ready to render, call `render_chart` with:\n"

   110→        "Do NOT output a JSON code block. Use the tool.\n"

   135→def _build_message_with_history(
   136→    message: str, conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None
   137→) -> str:
   138→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
   139→    if not conversation_history:
   140→        return message
   141→
   142→    history_text = "Previous conversation (for context, I am now editing a message):\n"
   143→    for entry in conversation_history:
   144→        role = entry.get("role", "user").capitalize()
   145→        content = entry.get("content", "")
   146→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
   147→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
   148→    return history_text
   149→
   150→
   151→
   152→def _build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages(
   153→    msg: dict, tool_names: dict[str, str], subagent_chart_specs: dict
   154→) -> None:
   155→    """Inspect an assistant message and capture render_chart input as chart_spec.
   156→
   157→    When the chart-builder subagent calls render_chart, the tool_use block
   158→    appears in an assistant message whose parent_tool_use_id is the Task
   159→    tool_use_id for the chart-builder.  We extract input directly so we never
   160→    need to parse free-text JSON.
   161→
   162→    A single chart-builder may call render_chart multiple times (e.g. for a
   163→    multi-chart request).  We accumulate all specs in a list keyed by the
   164→    parent task tool_use_id.
   165→    """
   166→    parent_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   167→    if not parent_id or tool_names.get(parent_id) != "chart-builder":
   168→        return
   169→    _DATA_FIELDS = ("x", "y", "z", "values", "labels", "lat", "lon", "r", "theta",
   170→                     "lowerfence", "q1", "median", "q3", "upperfence")
   171→    for block in msg.get("message", {}).get("content", []):
   172→        if block.get("type") == "tool_use" and "render_chart" in block.get("name", ""):
   173→            spec = block.get("input", {})
   174→            # Skip specs with all-empty data traces to avoid rendering blank charts.
   175→            traces = spec.get("data", [])
   176→            has_data = any(
   177→                isinstance(trace.get(f), list) and len(trace.get(f)) > 0
   178→                for trace in traces if isinstance(trace, dict)
   179→                for f in _DATA_FIELDS
   180→            )
   181→            if not has_data:
   182→                return
   183→            subagent_chart_specs.setdefault(parent_id, []).append(spec)
   184→            return
   185→

   187→async def stream_chat(
   188→    message: str,
   189→    session_id: str | None = None,
   190→    db: Database | None = None,
   191→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   192→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   193→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   194→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   195→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events via containerized sidecar."""
   196→    from app.container_manager import container_manager
   197→    if container_manager is None:
   198→        raise RuntimeError("Docker is not available. Container mode requires Docker.")
   199→    if db is None:
   200→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   201→
   202→    import httpx
   203→    import asyncio
   204→
   205→    # Only bake history into message text for edit/delete (no session to resume).
   206→    # For normal follow-ups with a session_id, pass history as a separate field
   207→    # so the sidecar can use it as fallback if resume fails.
   208→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history) if not session_id else message
   209→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   210→
   211→    # Pass Langfuse credentials to the container so the sidecar's
   212→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   213→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   214→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   215→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   216→        "SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS": str(SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   217→    }
   218→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   219→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   220→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   221→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   222→
   223→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   224→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   225→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   226→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   227→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   228→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   229→
   230→    try:
   231→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   232→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   233→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   234→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   235→
   236→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   237→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and
   238→        # can continue flushing keepalives to the client during startup.
   239→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   240→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   241→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   242→
   243→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   244→        elapsed = 0.0
   245→        while not create_future.done():
   246→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   247→            elapsed += 2.0
   248→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   249→                create_future.cancel()
   250→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   251→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   252→        info = await create_future
   253→        container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   254→
   255→        # Wait for container to be ready
   256→        for attempt in range(10):
   257→            try:
   258→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   259→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   260→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   261→                        break
   262→            except Exception:
   263→                pass
   264→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   265→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   266→        else:
   267→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   268→

   274→            "mcp_server_url": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",

   345→
   346→                    msg_type = msg.get("type")
   347→                    # --- Token-level streaming events from SDK ---
   348→                    if msg_type == "stream_event":
   349→                        event = msg.get("event", {})
   350→                        event_type = event.get("type", "")
   351→                        stream_parent = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   352→                        is_subagent_event = bool(stream_parent and stream_parent in tool_names)
   353→
   354→                        if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   355→                            delta = event.get("delta", {})
   356→                            delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   357→                            if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   358→                                text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   359→                                if text and not is_subagent_event:
   360→                                    yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   361→                            elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   362→                                text = delta.get("text", "")
   363→                                if text:
   364→                                    # Accumulate subagent text for chart_spec
   365→                                    # extraction (the SDK may not yield a
   366→                                    # complete assistant message for the
   367→                                    # subagent's final turn).
   368→                                    if is_subagent_event:
   369→                                        subagent_texts[stream_parent] = subagent_texts.get(stream_parent, "") + text
   370→                                    else:
   371→                                        event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
   372→                                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   373→
   374→                        elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   375→                            block = event.get("content_block", {})
   376→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   377→                            if block_type == "thinking":
   378→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   379→                                    has_thinking = True
   380→                            elif block_type == "text":
   381→                                if has_thinking and not is_subagent_event:
   382→                                    yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   383→                            elif block_type == "tool_use":
   384→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   385→                                    has_thinking = False
   386→                                    has_tool_calls = True
   387→
   388→                    # --- Complete assistant message (contains tool_use blocks) ---
   389→                    elif msg_type == "assistant":
   390→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   391→                        parent_tool_use_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   392→
   393→                        # Capture text from subagent assistant messages.
   394→                        # These carry the real subagent output (e.g. chart JSON)
   395→                        # that the Task tool_result metadata lacks.
   396→                        if parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in tool_names:
   397→                            text_parts = []
   398→                            for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   399→                                if block.get("type") == "text" and block.get("text"):
   400→                                    text_parts.append(block["text"])
   401→                            if text_parts:
   402→                                subagent_texts[parent_tool_use_id] = "\n".join(text_parts)
   403→                        _build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages(msg, tool_names, subagent_chart_specs)
   404→
   405→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   406→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   407→                            if block_type == "tool_use":
   408→                                has_tool_calls = True
   409→                                tool_id = block.get("id", "")
   410→                                tool_name = block.get("name", "")
   411→                                tool_input = block.get("input", {})
   412→                                tool_names[tool_id] = tool_name
   413→                                is_execute_sql = "execute_sql" in tool_name
   414→                                sql = tool_input.get("sql", "") if is_execute_sql else ""
   415→                                if sql:
   416→                                    tool_sqls[tool_id] = sql
   417→                                tool_call_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": tool_name}
   418→                                if sql:
   419→                                    tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   420→                                else:
   421→                                    tool_call_data["input"] = tool_input
   422→                                yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   423→                                if tool_name == "Task":
   424→                                    subagent_name = tool_input.get("subagent_type", "unknown")
   425→                                    subagent_prompt = tool_input.get("prompt", "")
   426→                                    tool_names[tool_id] = subagent_name
   427→                                    yield f"event: subagent_start\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': tool_id, 'name': subagent_name, 'prompt': subagent_prompt})}\n\n"
   428→
   429→                                # Detect ask_user_question tool
   430→                                if "ask_user_question" in tool_name:
   431→                                    from app.pending_questions import pending_question_store
   432→                                    import asyncio as _asyncio
   433→                                    for _ in range(50):
   434→                                        pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(stable_session)
   435→                                        if pending:
   436→                                            yield f"event: user_question\ndata: {json.dumps({'question_id': pending['question_id'], **pending['data']})}\n\n"

   438→                                            break
   439→                                        await _asyncio.sleep(0.1)

   441→                    # --- Tool results from user messages ---
   442→                    elif msg_type == "user":
   443→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})

   445→                        # tool_use_result.content (a list of content blocks).
   446→                        # Extract it so we can use it for chart_spec extraction.
   447→                        tool_use_result = msg.get("tool_use_result")
   448→                        tool_use_result_text = ""
   449→                        if isinstance(tool_use_result, dict):
   450→                            tur_content = tool_use_result.get("content")
   451→                            if isinstance(tur_content, list):
   452→                                parts = []
   453→                                for item in tur_content:
   454→                                    if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
   455→                                        parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
   456→                                tool_use_result_text = "\n".join(parts)
   457→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   458→                            if block.get("type") != "tool_result":
   459→                                continue
   460→                            tool_id = block.get("tool_use_id", "")
   461→                            name = tool_names.get(tool_id, "")
   462→                            content_parts = block.get("content", [])
   463→                            text = ""
   464→                            if isinstance(content_parts, list):
   465→                                for part in content_parts:
   466→                                    if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "text":
   467→                                        text = part.get("text", "")
   468→                            elif isinstance(content_parts, str):
   469→                                text = content_parts
   470→
   471→                            # Try to parse structured MCP result
   472→                            result_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   473→                            # Include the SQL from the original tool_call
   474→                            original_sql = tool_sqls.get(tool_id, "")
   475→                            if original_sql:
   476→                                result_data["sql"] = original_sql
   477→                            try:
   478→                                parsed = json.loads(text)
   479→                                if parsed.get("status") == "success":
   480→                                    if "chart_spec" in parsed:
   481→                                        result_data["chart_spec"] = parsed["chart_spec"]
   482→                                    else:
   483→                                        result_data["columns"] = parsed.get("columns", [])
   484→                                        result_data["rows"] = parsed.get("rows", [])[:100]
   485→                                        result_data["rowCount"] = parsed.get("rowCount", 0)
   486→                                elif parsed.get("status") == "error":
   487→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed.get("error", "")
   488→                                else:
   489→                                    result_data["output"] = text
   490→                            except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   491→                                result_data["output"] = text
   492→                            if block.get("is_error"):
   493→                                try:
   494→                                    parsed_err = json.loads(text)
   495→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed_err.get("error", text)
   496→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   497→                                    result_data["error"] = text
   498→                            # Detect subagent result (Task tool)
   499→                            if name in ("sql-analyst", "chart-builder"):
   500→                                end_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   501→                                if name == "chart-builder":
   502→                                    chart_specs = subagent_chart_specs.get(tool_id, [])
   503→                                    if chart_specs:
   504→                                        end_data["chart_specs"] = chart_specs
   505→                                    else:
   506→                                        logger.warning(
   507→                                            "[container] render_chart tool_use not found for chart-builder %s",
   508→                                            tool_id,
   509→                                        )
   510→                                yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   511→                                continue
   512→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   513→
   514→                    # --- Final result ---
   515→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   516→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   517→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   518→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   519→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   520→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   521→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   522→                        done_sent = True
   523→
   524→                    # --- Sidecar error (e.g. SDK/CLI crash inside container) ---
   525→                    elif msg_type == "error":
   526→                        error_text = msg.get("message") or "Sidecar error"
   527→                        logger.error("Sidecar reported error: %s", error_text)
   528→                        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   529→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   530→                        done_sent = True
   531→
   532→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   533→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   534→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   535→                        if sys_session:
   536→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   537→
   538→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   539→        if not done_sent:
   540→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   541→            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': 'Connection to agent was lost. Please try again.'})}\n\n"
   542→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   543→
   544→    except Exception as e:
   545→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   546→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   547→    # Note: Container is kept alive for session resume (--resume flag) and is
   548→    # cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   549→    # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.
   550→

     1→import express, { Request, Response } from "express";

     9→// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.
    10→// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we
    11→// create the expected structure at startup.
    12→const claudeDir = join(homedir(), ".claude");
    13→for (const sub of ["debug", "projects"]) {
    14→  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);
    15→  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    16→    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    17→  }
    18→}
    19→const settingsFile = join(claudeDir, "remote-settings.json");
    20→if (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {
    21→  writeFileSync(settingsFile, "{}");
    22→}
    23→
    24→// Initialize Langfuse if credentials are available (reads from env vars
    25→// LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL automatically)
    26→let langfuse: Langfuse | null = null;
    27→if (process.env.LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY && process.env.LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY) {
    28→  langfuse = new Langfuse();
    29→  console.log("[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled");
    30→}
    31→
    32→const app = express();
    33→app.use(express.json({ limit: "10mb" }));
    34→
    35→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
    36→
    37→// Per-request abort tracking — avoids a global variable whose cleanup in one
    38→// request's `finally` block can clobber a concurrently-started request.
    39→let nextRequestId = 0;
    40→const activeAborts = new Map<number, AbortController>();
    41→
    42→// How long (ms) to wait for the SDK iterator to yield a message before
    43→// aborting.  Resets on every message so active multi-turn queries are not
    44→// interrupted.  Covers cases where the CLI subprocess hangs on startup
    45→// (e.g. broken session resume, unreachable MCP server).
    46→const SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = parseInt(process.env.SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS || "120000", 10);
    47→
    48→// Timeout (ms) for pre-flight reachability checks against MCP / API URLs.
    49→const PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000; // 10 seconds
    50→
    51→app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    52→  const response: HealthResponse = { status: "ok" };
    53→  res.json(response);
    54→});
    55→
    56→app.post("/query", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    57→  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;
    58→
    59→  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {
    60→    res.status(400).json({ error: "message and system_prompt are required" });
    61→    return;
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  // Set up SSE headers
    65→  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
    66→  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
    67→  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
    68→  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
    69→  res.flushHeaders();
    70→
    71→  const abortController = new AbortController();
    72→  const requestId = nextRequestId++;
    73→  activeAborts.set(requestId, abortController);
    74→
    75→  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. Bifrost gateway URLs) with process env
    76→  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};
    77→  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
    78→    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;
    79→  }
    80→  if (body.env) {
    81→    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);
    82→  }
    83→  // Scrub Langfuse credentials so the agent subprocess cannot read them from
    84→  // the inherited environment — matches backend subprocess behaviour.
    85→  // The sidecar's own Langfuse client already holds the credentials internally.
    86→  sdkEnv["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = "";
    87→  sdkEnv["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = "";
    88→
    89→  let responseEnded = false;
    90→
    91→  // Detect client disconnect
    92→  res.on("close", () => {
    93→    if (!responseEnded) {
    94→      abortController.abort();
    95→    }
    96→  });
    97→
    98→  // --- Langfuse tracing setup ---
    99→  // Mirror the backend's session ID handling:
   100→  //   - langfuse_session_id (from frontend for edit/delete) takes priority
   101→  //   - session_id (CLI session from previous turn) as fallback
   102→  const modelName = body.model || process.env.ORCHESTRATOR_MODEL || "claude-sonnet-4-6";
   103→  const traceMessage = body.original_message || body.message;
   104→  const traceInput: Record<string, unknown> = {
   105→    message: traceMessage.substring(0, 500),
   106→  };
   107→  if (body.conversation_history && body.conversation_history.length > 0) {
   108→    traceInput.conversation_history = body.conversation_history;
   109→  }
   110→  const trace = langfuse?.trace({
   111→    name: "agent-chat",
   112→    sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined,
   113→    input: traceInput,
   114→    metadata: { model: modelName, mode: "container" },
   115→  });
   116→
   117→  // Per-turn usage tracking from stream events
   118→  let currentGenUsage: { input?: number; output?: number } = {};
   119→  // Accumulated messages for generation input context
   120→  const accumulatedMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [

   123→
   124→  // Collect stderr from the CLI subprocess for debugging
   125→  const stderrLines: string[] = [];
   126→
   127→  // Declared here so `finally` can clear it even if `try` throws early
   128→  let idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

   130→  try {
   131→    // --- Pre-flight reachability checks ---

   133→    // server or the Bifrost LLM gateway.  Test connectivity first so we can
   134→    // fail fast with a useful error message.
   135→    const apiBase = sdkEnv["ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"] || "";
   136→    console.log(
   137→      `[sidecar] reqId=${requestId} mcp_url=${body.mcp_server_url || "(none)"} api_base=${apiBase} session_id=${body.session_id || "(none)"}`
   138→    );
   139→
   140→    if (body.mcp_server_url) {
   141→      try {
   142→        const mcpResp = await fetch(body.mcp_server_url, {
   143→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS),
   144→        });
   145→        // SSE endpoints normally return 200 with text/event-stream; any
   146→        // non-error status is fine — we just need to know the host is up.
   147→        mcpResp.body?.cancel(); // don't consume the stream
   148→        console.log(`[sidecar] MCP reachability OK (status=${mcpResp.status})`);
   149→      } catch (e: unknown) {
   150→        const reason = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   151→        throw new Error(
   152→          `MCP server unreachable at ${body.mcp_server_url}: ${reason}. ` +
   153→          `Check that BACKEND_BASE_URL is reachable from inside the container.`
   154→        );
   155→      }
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    if (apiBase) {
   159→      try {
   160→        // Just a quick TCP-level check — Bifrost will return 4xx without
   161→        // a real API key but that still proves reachability.
   162→        const apiResp = await fetch(`${apiBase}/v1/models`, {
   163→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS),
   164→        });
   165→        apiResp.body?.cancel();
   166→        console.log(`[sidecar] Bifrost LLM gateway reachability OK (status=${apiResp.status})`);
   167→      } catch (e: unknown) {
   168→        const reason = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   169→        throw new Error(
   170→          `Bifrost LLM gateway unreachable at ${apiBase}: ${reason}. ` +
   171→          `Check that BIFROST_BASE_URL is reachable from inside the container.`
   172→        );

   175→
   176→    // Convert agents payload from the backend into SDK AgentDefinition objects.
   177→    // The SDK only accepts 'sonnet' | 'opus' | 'haiku' | 'inherit' for model.
   178→    // Arbitrary strings (e.g. "openai/gpt-5.2") are silently rejected, causing
   179→    // the agent to not be registered.  Validate and fall back to "inherit".
   180→    const VALID_AGENT_MODELS = new Set(["sonnet", "opus", "haiku", "inherit"]);
   181→    let sdkAgents: Record<string, AgentDefinition> | undefined;

   184→      for (const [name, def] of Object.entries(body.agents)) {

   192→          model,

   194→      }

   197→    // Common SDK options (without resume/prompt — those vary on retry)
   198→    const baseOptions = {

   208→      stderr: (line: string) => {

   211→      },
   212→      ...(sdkAgents ? { agents: sdkAgents } : {}),
   213→      ...(body.mcp_server_url
   214→        ? {

   225→    // Resume fallback: try with resume first. If the SDK yields a "result"

   246→      idleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   247→        console.error(`[sidecar] SDK idle timeout (${SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS}ms) reached, aborting reqId=${requestId}`);

   250→
   251→      let resumeFailedNeedsRetry = false;

   270→              if ((resultText + " " + errors).includes("No conversation found")
   271→                  && body.conversation_history && body.conversation_history.length > 0) {
   272→                console.log(`[sidecar] Resume failed ("No conversation found"), will retry with history reqId=${requestId}`);
   273→                resumeFailedNeedsRetry = true;
   274→                break; // Don't forward this error to the client
   275→              }
   276→            }
   277→          }
   278→
   279→          // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
   280→          res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
   281→
   282→          // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---
   283→          if (!trace) continue;
   284→          const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
   285→
   286→          if (msg.type === "stream_event") {
   287→            const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   288→            const eventType = event.type as string;
   289→            if (eventType === "message_start") {
   290→              const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   291→              const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   292→              currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };
   293→            } else if (eventType === "message_delta") {
   294→              const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   295→              currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;
   296→            }
   297→          } else if (msg.type === "assistant") {
   298→            const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   299→            const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];
   300→            const usage =
   301→              currentGenUsage.input !== undefined
   302→                ? {
   303→                    input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,
   304→                    output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,
   305→                    total: (currentGenUsage.input || 0) + (currentGenUsage.output || 0),
   306→                    unit: "TOKENS" as const,
   307→                  }
   308→                : undefined;
   309→            const gen = trace.generation({
   310→              name: "claude.assistant.turn",
   311→              model: (msgObj.model as string) || modelName,
   312→              input: { messages: accumulatedMessages.slice(-6) },
   313→              output: { content, role: "assistant" },
   314→              usage,
   315→            });
   316→            gen.end();
   317→            currentGenUsage = {};
   318→            accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content });
   319→          } else if (msg.type === "user") {
   320→            const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   321→            accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "user", content: msgObj.content });
   322→          } else if (msg.type === "result") {
   323→            const actualSessionId = (msg.session_id as string | undefined) || body.session_id;
   324→            const traceSessionId = body.langfuse_session_id || actualSessionId;
   325→            trace.update({
   326→              ...(traceSessionId ? { sessionId: traceSessionId } : {}),
   327→              output: { session_id: actualSessionId },
   328→            });
   329→          } else if (msg.type === "system") {
   330→            const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;
   331→            if (sessionId && !body.langfuse_session_id) {
   332→              trace.update({ sessionId });
   333→            }
   334→          }
   335→        } // end for-await
   336→      } catch (innerErr: unknown) {
   337→        // If we're retrying due to resume failure, swallow the cleanup error
   338→        // (e.g., "Claude Code process exited with code 1" after session not found)
   339→        if (resumeFailedNeedsRetry) {
   340→          console.log(`[sidecar] Swallowing post-resume error during retry: ${innerErr instanceof Error ? innerErr.message : String(innerErr)}`);
   341→        } else {
   342→          throw innerErr;
   343→        }
   344→      }
   345→
   346→      if (resumeFailedNeedsRetry && !retried) {
   347→        retried = true;
   348→        useResume = false;
   349→        let messageWithHistory = "Previous conversation (for context):\n";
   350→        for (const entry of body.conversation_history!) {
   351→          const role = (entry.role || "user").charAt(0).toUpperCase() + (entry.role || "user").slice(1);
   352→          messageWithHistory += `\n${role}: ${entry.content}\n`;
   353→        }
   354→        messageWithHistory += `\n---\n\nMy new message:\n${body.message}`;
   355→        currentPrompt = messageWithHistory;
   356→        stderrLines.length = 0;
   357→        if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); idleTimer = null; }
   358→        continue; // retry the queryLoop
   359→      }
   360→
   361→      break; // success or non-retryable error — exit queryLoop
   362→    } // end queryLoop
   363→  } catch (err: unknown) {
   364→    let errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   365→    // Append CLI stderr for context when the process crashes
   366→    if (stderrLines.length > 0) {
   367→      errMsg += ` | CLI stderr: ${stderrLines.slice(-5).join(" ")}`;
   368→    }
   369→    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect
   370→    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError")) {
   371→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);
   372→      if (!responseEnded) {
   373→        res.write(
   374→          `data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: errMsg })}\n\n`
   375→        );
   376→      }
   377→      if (trace) {
   378→        trace.update({ output: { error: errMsg } });
   379→      }
   380→    }
   381→  } finally {
   382→    if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); }
   383→    activeAborts.delete(requestId);
   384→    responseEnded = true;
   385→    res.end();
   386→    // Flush Langfuse events before the response is fully closed
   387→    if (langfuse) {
   388→      await langfuse.flushAsync().catch(() => {});
   389→    }
   390→    console.log(`[sidecar] SSE stream ended reqId=${requestId}`);
   391→  }
   392→});
   393→
   394→app.post("/stop", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
   395→  if (activeAborts.size > 0) {
   396→    for (const [id, controller] of activeAborts) {
   397→      controller.abort();
   398→      activeAborts.delete(id);
   399→    }
   400→    res.json({ status: "stopped" });
   401→  } else {
   402→    res.json({ status: "no_active_session" });
   403→  }
   404→});
   405→
   406→app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
   407→  console.log(`Sidecar agent server listening on port ${PORT}`);
   408→});
   409→
```

    12→/**
    13→ * Strip chart_spec JSON code blocks from answer text.
    14→ * The orchestrator may include the raw chart spec JSON even though the chart
    15→ * is rendered separately. Remove ```json ... ``` blocks containing chart_spec
    16→ * or Plotly trace data so they don't appear as raw text.
    17→ */
    18→function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
    19→  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();
    20→}
    21→
    22→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder: string | undefined, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    23→  // Use streaming remainder if available
    24→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    25→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    26→    const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    27→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    28→  }
    29→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    30→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    31→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    32→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    33→      const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    34→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    35→    }
    36→  }
    37→  return t('thinking');
    38→}
    39→
    40→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isThinkingPhase, isAgentStreaming, hasAnswer }: {

    55→  ) || streamingRemainder?.trim();
    56→
    57→  if (!hasContent) return null;
    58→

    61→
    62→  return (
    63→    <details className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--thinking message-bubble__collapsible" open={isThinkingPhase || undefined}>
    64→      <summary className="message-bubble__collapsible-summary">

    69→        {thinkingSegments.map((seg, i) => {

   130→export function MessageBubble({ message, messageIndex }: { message: ChatMessage; messageIndex: number }) {

   343→              {isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {

   345→                return displayText?.trim() ? (

   356→            return displayText?.trim() ? (

   366→            const lastUserMsg = messages.slice().reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'user');
   367→            const handleRetry = lastUserMsg ? () => sendMessage(lastUserMsg.content) : undefined;
   368→            return <ErrorBlock key={`error-${i}`} errorMessage={seg.errorMessage || 'Unknown error'} onRetry={!isStreaming ? handleRetry : undefined} />;
   369→          })}
   370→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && questionSegments.length === 0 && (
   371→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   372→          )}
   373→        </div>
   374→      ) : (
   375→        <>
   376→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   377→            {message.content ? (
   378→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{message.content}</ReactMarkdown>
   379→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   380→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   381→            ) : null}
   382→          </div>
   383→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   384→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   385→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   386→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   387→              ))}
   388→            </div>
   389→          )}
   390→        </>
   391→      )}
   392→    </div>
   393→  );
   394→}
   395→
```

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   105→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   106→  userSessionId?: string,
   107→): Promise<void> {
   108→  try {
   109→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   110→      method: 'POST',
   111→      headers: {
   112→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   113→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   114→      },
   115→      body: JSON.stringify({
   116→        message,
   117→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   118→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   119→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   120→      }),
   121→      signal,
   122→    });
   123→
   124→    if (!response.ok) {
   125→      const errorText = await response.text();
   126→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   127→      return;
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   131→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   132→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   133→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   134→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   135→  }
   136→}
   137→
   138→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   139→  newMessage: string,
   140→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   141→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   142→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   143→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   144→  userSessionId?: string,
   145→): Promise<void> {
   146→  try {
   147→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {
   148→      method: 'POST',
   149→      headers: {
   150→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   151→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   152→      },
   153→      body: JSON.stringify({
   154→        new_message: newMessage,
   155→        conversation_history: conversationHistory,
   156→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   157→      }),
   158→      signal,
   159→    });
   160→
   161→    if (!response.ok) {
   162→      const errorText = await response.text();
   163→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   164→      return;
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   168→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   169→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   170→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   171→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   172→  }
   173→}
   174→
   175→function handleSSEEvent(
   176→  eventType: string,
   177→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   178→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   179→) {
   180→  switch (eventType) {
   181→    case 'thinking':
   182→    case 'answer':
   183→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   184→      break;
   185→    case 'thinking_done':
   186→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   187→      break;
   188→    case 'tool_call':
   189→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   190→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   191→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   192→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   193→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   194→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   195→        columns: [],
   196→        rows: [],
   197→        rowCount: 0,
   198→      });
   199→      break;
   200→    case 'tool_result': {
   201→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   202→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   203→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   204→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   205→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   206→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   207→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   208→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   209→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   210→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   211→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   212→      };
   213→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   214→      break;
   215→    }
   216→    case 'subagent_start':
   217→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   218→        id: data.id as string,
   219→        name: data.name as string,
   220→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   221→      });
   222→      break;
   223→    case 'subagent_end': {
   224→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   225→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   226→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   227→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   228→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   229→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   230→
   231→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   232→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   233→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   234→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   235→            id: data.id as string,
   236→            name: data.name as string,
   237→            chart_spec: spec,
   238→          });
   239→        }
   240→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   241→        if (data.result) {
   242→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   243→            id: data.id as string,
   244→            name: data.name as string,
   245→            result: data.result as string,
   246→          });
   247→        }
   248→      } else {
   249→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   250→          id: data.id as string,
   251→          name: data.name as string,
   252→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   253→        });
   254→      }
   255→      break;
   256→    }
   257→    case 'user_question': {
   258→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   259→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   260→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   261→        (opt: unknown) => {
   262→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   263→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   264→          return { label: String(opt) };
   265→        }
   266→      );
   267→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   268→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   269→        question: data.question as string,
   270→        options: normalizedOptions,
   271→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   272→      });
   273→      break;
   274→    }
   275→    case 'done':
   276→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   277→      break;
   278→    case 'error':
   279→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   280→      break;
   281→  }
   282→}
   283→
